Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the systems object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 659. The moduli space has dimension at most 41 with 2 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 659 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the systems structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 41. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
